# System: documented functions (`system`)

Reference table generated in the documentation round of 2026-10-31 and extended in the rounds of 2026-11-04. Each row is a function that reached the `documented` stage (`docs/PROGRESS.md`): its prototype, purpose, return value, side effects and callers, read from the Ghidra decompilation with the names of that round. The same text is the function's plate comment in Ghidra (`re/documented*.tsv`, applied by `tools/ghidra/ApplySignatures.java`). How the subsystem works as a whole is in [`../system.md`](../system.md); pointers to structures are typed `void *` and the structure is named in the summary.

Functions: 1376 (BOOT.BIN 1070, rel_blackjack 1, rel_connection 1, rel_conv_machine 2, rel_cutin_viewer 4, rel_debug_menu 42, rel_duel_draw 21, rel_duel_eng 2, rel_duel_mgr 37, rel_field 6, rel_html_view 16, rel_password 2, rel_story 3, rel_title 3, rel_umd_replace 74, rel_usb_connect 92).


## `BOOT.BIN`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x08804000` | `int system_sys_open(char *path, uint flags)` | newlib _open syscall over the PSP I/O manager: translates the newlib O_* flags (access mode 0/1/2 to PSP_O_RDONLY 1 / WRONLY 2 / RDWR 3, O_APPEND 0x8 to 0x100, keeps 0x200/0x400/0x800 = CREAT/TRUNC/EXCL, O_NONBLOCK 0x4000 to 0x4) and calls sceIoOpen(path, flags, 0x1a4 = mode 0644). Returns the sceIoOpen result (file descriptor or negative error) through the tail of the call. Side effects: opens a file. No direct caller (newlib syscall layer, unused by the game). |
| `0x08804098` | `int system_sys_lseek(int fd, int offset, int whence)` | newlib _lseek syscall: maps descriptors 0/1/2 to sceKernelStdin/Stdout/Stderr, returns 0xfffffff7 (-9) for a negative descriptor, otherwise forwards to sceIoLseek; the forwarding of offset and whence is hidden by the decompiler (passed on as the sceIoLseek offset and whence, hypothesis, confidence medium). Returns the new position or a negative error. Side effects: moves the file position. Callers: 1 (system_lseek_r). |
| `0x088041c8` | `void system_sys_exit(int status)` | newlib _exit: when the module reentrancy struct (system_get_module_reent) is not the calling thread's (system_get_reent) it prints a libc error with sceKernelPrintf and ends only the thread (sceKernelExitThread(status)); otherwise reclaims the reentrancy struct (system_reclaim_reent), unregisters it (system_unregister_module_reent, which returns the owning module id) and, when that module is not the current one, stops and unloads it and exits the thread; else calls sceKernelStopUnloadSelfModuleWithStatus(1, 0, 0, 0, 0). Does not return. Callers: 1 (system_exit). |
| `0x08804290` | `uint system_sys_sbrk(int increment)` | newlib _sbrk: on the first call allocates the break area from user partition 2 (sceKernelAllocPartitionMemory, name "UserSbrk", type 3, size = KiB count at 0x0889ed5c * 0x400, alignment 0x1000), storing the block id at 0x08916ddc, the area start at 0x08916dd4, the end at 0x08916dd8 and the current break at 0x08916dd0. Moves the break by increment when it stays inside [start, end]. Returns the old break, or 0xffffffff when the area cannot be created (size 0) or the new break would leave it. Side effects: the globals 0x08916dd0-0x08916ddc, partition allocation. Callers: 2 (system_user_main_thread, system_sbrk_r). |

A return chooses the first state by the scene returned from: story 2 -> 5 (arg 0) or 3; deck editor 4 -> 0x1a when (arg & 3) == 2, else 9 with 5; duel manager 6 -> 5 for setup mode 5, 0x18 with the word 0x08ac2574 for mode 2 (tutorial list), else 0xf (game-global flag 4 set, wireless menu flags) or 9; download 0xe -> 9 with 7 (0x08eeac74 = 7); the minigame scenes 0xf-0x1d -> 9 with 6, usb 0x17 / umd 0x1b -> 9 with 7 (0x08eeac74 = 6), gallery 0x1e -> 9 with 7 (0x08eeac74 = 2) (ui_title_start_with_pending_state). Other transition types set 0x08eea3dc = 0x2d. Installs the frame callback (ui_title_call_draw, leave ui_title_release_work) and runs ui_title_update_state each frame until it returns 0x2e. Side effects: title state, frame callback. No direct caller (scene start registered by system_title_register_scene). |
